# Original Pro assets

Source: [Pro conversation](https://chatgpt.com/c/6ae6c01c-babc-83e9-bf65-d92651191324).

Preserved 2457 original files without byte changes. All 2457 supplied manifest hashes passed before collection.

The package includes 197 coverage entries, 105 visual families, four nonvisual groups, 323 positive source companions, 359 SVGs, and 34 recommendations.

The supplied input source ZIP is excluded. It remains in ignored working storage with the result ZIP. The original package manifest still names that omitted input.

Read [summary.md](summary.md), [implementation-plan.md](implementation-plan.md), and [dispositions.md](dispositions.md) for reviewed conclusions. The original reports describe the pinned baseline, not new shipped behavior.
